# Can you please modify the now playing bar in the ui so it can show little boxes for runners

and maybe some sort of colour indicator of health

The headline fix: the `showRunnerBoxes` kill switch was documented but unreachable — it was never registered in `DEFAULT_UI_PREFERENCES`/`BOOLEAN_PREFERENCE_KEYS`, so sanitization dropped it on load, `updatePreference` refused it as unknown, and import/export ignored it. I registered it (default `true`, no migration needed), added a Workspace-tab toggle in [UiPreferencesPanel.jsx](/home/server/.kainbain/runner-work/kb-b5716327-d950-4e3d-b430-4a1c15a75e66/frontend/src/components/settings/UiPreferencesPanel.jsx), and mirrored it in the test mock. Hardening: new shared `selectRenderableRunnerEntries` filter used by both the visibility gate and the row (they can't disagree — junk entries fall back to the legacy view instead of phantom \"unknown\" boxes), index-suffixed row keys against duplicate runner ids, blank id/label fail-closed to \"unknown\", trimmed aria labels, and numeric-timestamp support with blank-stamp fall-through in recency ordering. 12 new maintained tests; `NowPlayingBar.jsx` itself needed no change.","meta":{"source":"process-log","runnerId":"local-alpha","executionId":"c4fb6759-1d6b-4a1d-b76a-cbe01d8f55a7","seq":74}},{"id":"log-7779","ts":"2026-10-27T22:46:39.550Z","feed":"main","feeds":["main"],"kind":"runner","turn":null,"text":"[runner local-alpha] Verification observed: focused files 103/103 pass; full frontend suite 284 files / 3849 tests pass; `check-one-function-per-file`, `check-jsdoc-example`, `check-duplicate-settings` all pass; frontend lint 0 errors with no new warnings.
